Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on interpolation structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 198. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 359 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on interpolation structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 198. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 359. QED.
